```
import time
import math
import numpy as np

def _as_valid_float_list(x):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.isfinite(arr).all():
        return None
    if float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
        return None
    return arr

def _score_list(list):
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(list)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return float('inf')
    return float(s)

def _update(best, best_score, cand_arr):
    # returns updated
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 1.5)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best = [1.0]
    best_score = _score_list(best)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score = 2.0

    def consider(arr):
        nonlocal best, best_score
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline: return
        if arr is None: return
        arr = np.clip(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return
        # optional reduce length? no
        try:
            cand = [float(v) for v in arr]
        except Exception: return
        if sum(cand) < 0.01: return
        s = _score_list(cand)
        if s < best_score:
            best_score = s; best = cand
```

   - But nonconvex; use many steps and random restarts.

Wait if j valid and m-j valid, both terms are present (i=j and i=m-j); they are same a_j*a_{m-j}, but if j≠m-j they are two distinct products (a_j a_{m-j} and a_{m-j} a_j). Internal objective for DE:
```
def coarse_obj(v, n, K, x_fine, method):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    if not finite or sum<1e-12: return 1e6
    a=np.interp(x_fine, linspace(0,1,K), v)  # if K=1, etc
    # maybe adjust to ensure endpoints not all? no
    return internal_score(a)
```
Interp allocation each eval. Precompute `x_fine=(np.arange(n)/ (n-1))`, `x_c=(np.arange(K)/(K-1))`.
If coarse length K=1, no; choose >=2.
Returning score with sum=1. We also might want add small smoothing to convolution? no.

After DE, we get `best_v` and best fine a. But DE objective uses internal score, may have slight FFT errors; final exact we validate.

Potential drawback: If DE finds a coarse v with internal score e.g., 1.49, but due interpolation/FFT max could be 1.5. Fine. We keep.

Now local refinement on fine vector length n (e.g., 1024, 2048). We can implement a `refine` routine:
- Input a (nonnegative length n with sum maybe normalized to 1). We'll keep normalized sum=1 to simplify; score=2n*maxb.
- Compute active lags and subgradient descent.
Pseudo:
```
def refine(a, budget_deadline, max_iters=2000):
    a = a / a.sum()
    best=a.copy(); best_f=internal_score(a)
    eta=0.1
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       b=convolve(a,a) # FFT
       M=np.max(b)
       # active mask = b >= M - 1e-6*M maybe top r
       active = np.where(b >= M - 0.05*M)[0]
       # If too many, top 8 lags
       if len(active)>16: active = top 16 indices of b
       g=np.zeros(n)
       for m in active:
           lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
           # derivative: 2*a[hi:lo reversed?]
           # For j in [lo,hi], if m-j in [0,n-1]; m-j ranges m-hi to m-lo, reversed.
           # We want g[j] += 2*a[m-j]
           g[lo:hi+1] += 2*a[m-hi:m-lo+1][::-1] (maybe use slicing)
       g -= np.mean(g)  # sum constraint
       # step
       for step in [eta, eta/2, eta/5, eta*2?] choose best
       a_trial = np.clip(a - step*g, 0, None)
       if a_trial.sum()<1e-12: continue
       a_trial /= a_trial.sum()
       f=internal_score(a_trial)
       if f<best_f: update; maybe eta *=1.05 else eta*=0.8
       if it%50==0: try random perturbation? 
    return best
```
But if active gradient is zero (constant), no movement. Starting from DE nonuniform, ok. Need ensure not increasing. Line search can evaluate multiple candidates each iter; 2000* maybe 5 evals too many. Limit.
- We can also use multiplicative update: `a_trial = a * exp(-eta*g)` where g normalized, then normalize/clipping automatic. This maintains positivity. For small eta, fine.
- Since objective is scale invariant and we work sum1, subtracting mean from gradient enforces sum approximately.
- Choose active top maybe one lag only for aggressive, but can overshoot. Use top 3-5 with weights by b values normalized.

Alternative local: random search around best using small Gaussian in log-space with norm controlled. Evaluate exact internal. This was previous program. For nonuniform shapes, random perturbations may find improvements. We can combine:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, scale):
   a2 = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,n)); clip; normalize
```
Check score. This is simple and time-controlled. Use decreasing scale. For n=1024, each eval FFT cheap. Can run thousands. It may explore. Use it as final phase. Also block perturbations as parent.

```

for m,w in zip(idx,weights):
   lo = max(0, m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
   # length L=hi-lo+1
   # m-j for j=lo..hi ranges m-hi .. m-lo decreasing
   seg = a[m-hi:m-lo+1][::-1]
   g[lo:hi+1] += w*2*seg
g -= g.mean()
```

Let's approximate continuous using polynomial moments.

Let's compute symbolic. Let P(s)=A+Bs+Cs^2. P(t-s)=A+B t -B s + C(t^2 -2ts+s^2)= (A+Bt+Ct^2) + (-B -2Ct)s + C s^2.
Product coefficients in s:
const: A*(A+Bt+Ct²)
s: A*(-B-2Ct) + B*(A+Bt+Ct²) = -AB-2ACt + AB + B^2 t + BC t² = t(B²-2AC)+BC t².
s²: A*C + B*(-B-2Ct)+C*C t² = AC -B² -2BCt + C²t².
Integrate 0..t:

Implement `_choose_conv(a, n_limit=1024)`:
```
def _max_conv(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 1024:
        b=np.convolve(a,a)
        return float(np.max(b))
    N=1
    need=2*n-1
    while N<need: N <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:need]
    # remove small negative
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if m < 0: m=0.0
    return m
```
For n=1025, FFT. Good.

```
def make_de_objective(n,K,seed):
   W = build_W(n,K)
   N = fft_len(n)
   def obj(v):
       v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return 1e6
       a = W @ v
       # if due numerical slight negative, clip
       if a.size ==0 or not finite: return 1e6
       # v in [0,1], a nonnegative but maybe -1e-16
       a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s = a.sum()
       if s <= 1e-12: return 1e6
       a = a / s
       F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
       b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:2*n-1]
       M = float(np.max(b))
       if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: return 1e6
       return 2.0*n*M
   return obj, W
```

def _refine(a, deadline, max_evals=2000, seed=1):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: return a
    a = a/s
    best=a.copy(); best_f=_score_norm(a); best_f_exact? internal
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    step_scale=1.0
    no_improve=0
    evals=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and evals<max_evals:
        # subgradient
        b = _max_conv? Need full b for active. Implement _convolve(a)
        b = _convolve_full(a)
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not finite: break
        Kact = 6 if n>2000 else 8
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -Kact)[-Kact:]
        w = b[idx].astype(float); w_sum=w.sum(); if w_sum>0: w/=w_sum
        g=np.zeros(n)
        for m,wt in zip(idx,w):
           lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
           if lo<=hi:
              g[lo:hi+1] += wt*2.0*a[m-hi:m-lo+1][::-1]
        g -= g.mean()
        gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
        if gmax>1e-15:
            gnorm = g/gmax
            base_steps = [0.05, 0.1, 0.2]
            best_trial=None; best_trial_f=best_f
            for ss in base_steps:
                step = ss*step_scale
                a2 = a * np.exp(-step*gnorm) # stays positive
                s2=a2.sum()
                if s2<1e-12: continue
                a2 /= s2
                f2=_score_norm(a2); evals+=1
                if f2 < best_trial_f - 1e-14:
                    best_trial = a2; best_trial_f=f2
            if best_trial is not None:
                a=best_trial.copy()
                if a is not best? update best
                if best_trial_f < best_f:
                    best=a.copy(); best_f=best_trial_f; no_improve=0; step_scale=min(3.0,step_scale*1.2)
                else:
                    no_improve+=1; step_scale=max(0.3,step_scale*0.8)
            else:
                # random perturbation
                for scale in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
                    a2 = a * np.exp(scale*rng.normal(size=n))
                    s2=a2.sum(); if <...
                    a2/=s2; f2=_score_norm(a2); evals+=1
                    if f2<best_trial_f: accept...
                no_improve +=1
                if no_improve>50: step_scale=1.0; no_improve=0
                if no_improve>200: break? 
    return best
```

def run_de(f, K, maxiter, popsize, seed):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*K
    kwargs = dict(maxiter=maxiter, popsize=popsize, mutation=(0.5,1.5), recombination=0.7, seed=seed, tol=1e-5, polish=False)
    try:
       return sopt.differential_evolution(f, bounds, init='latinhypercube', **kwargs)
    except TypeError:
       del kwargs['init']; return sopt.differential_evolution(f,bounds,**kwargs)
```

Pseudo code full:
```
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as sopt

_PENALTY = 1e100

def _fft_len(need):
    N=1
    while N < need: N <<=1
    return N

def _conv_full(a, use_fft_threshold=256):
    n=a.size
    if n <= use_fft_threshold:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=1
    need=2*n-1
    while N<need: N <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:need]
    # clean tiny negative? not for max
    return out

def _score_norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return _PENALTY
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return _PENALTY
    # assume normalized; but for safety, if sum not close 1? Could normalize here if not? This function called with normalized. To avoid error if not, we can not.
    m = float(np.max(_conv_full(a)))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <=0: return _PENALTY
    return 2.0*a.size*m
```
But `_score_norm` doesn't normalize; for exact candidates we normalize before. For safety, create `_score_any(a)` that normalizes and uses _score_norm. But DE objective normalizes then calls _score_norm to avoid double sum.

   return a/s
```

DE objective:
```
def _de_objective_factory(n, K, holder):
    # build W
    W = _build_interp_weights(n,K)
    need=2*n-1
    use_fft = n > 256
    if use_fft: N=_fft_len(need)
    def obj(v):
       v=np.asarray(v,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return _PENALTY
       a = W.dot(v)
       if a.size==0: return _PENALTY
       # due bounds nonneg, but clip
       np.maximum(a,0.0,out=a)
       s=a.sum()
       if s <= 1e-12: return _PENALTY
       a *= (1.0/s)
       if use_fft:
          F=np.fft.rfft(a,N); b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:need]
       else:
          b=np.convolve(a,a)
       m=float(np.max(b))
       if not np.isfinite(m) or m<=0: return _PENALTY
       f=2.0*n*m
       if f < holder[0]:
          holder[0]=f; holder[1]=a.copy()
       return f
    return obj
```
`holder` list [inf,None]. Updating a.copy inside objective can be somewhat costly but only when new best; okay. But if f lower by tiny many times, copies. Could threshold 1e-9. Fine.

Build W:
```
def _build_interp_weights(n,K):
   if K==1: return np.ones((n,1))
   t = np.linspace(0.0,float(K-1),n)  # positions in coarse index
   j=np.floor(t).astype(np.int64)
   j=np.minimum(j,K-1)
   frac=t-j
   # for j=K-1, frac should 0 because t max K-1; due floating may 0.
   W=np.zeros((n,K),dtype=np.float64)
   idx=np.arange(n)
   np.add.at(W,(idx,j),1.0-frac)
   j2=np.minimum(j+1,K-1)
   np.add.at(W,(idx,j2),frac)
   return W
```
For n not exactly matches K grid, okay. `np.linspace(0,K-1,n)` ensures endpoints exact. If K=16,n=4096, t has floats.

Consider candidate:
```
def make_consider(...):
   def consider(a, exact_now=False):
      nonlocal best_arr, best_f, exact_best_arr, exact_best_score
      a=np.asarray(a,float)
      if a.ndim!=1 or a.size==0: return
      if not finite: return
      a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
      s=a.sum()
      if s<0.01? For internal normalized, s can <.01 if n small? We normalize to 1; if original sum small, still can scale. Evaluator after scaling sum1. So if s<=1e-12 return.
      a=a/s
      f=_score_norm(a)
      if finite f < best_f -1e-12:
          best_f=f; best_arr=a.copy(); candidates append

Let's write `_refine` carefully:
```
def _refine(a, deadline, max_evals=2000, seed=0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size<4: return a
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return a
    a=a/s
    best=a.copy()
    best_f=_score_norm(best)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    step_scale=1.0
    fails=0
    evals=0
    # precompute N if using FFT? _score_norm does.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and evals < max_evals:
        # compute active gradient
        b=_conv_full(best)  # or current a? use best? If current is best (greedy), same.
        M=float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M): break
        n=best.size
        kact = 8 if n<2000 else 5
        idx=np.argpartition(b, -kact)[-kact:]
        w=b[idx]
        wsum=w.sum()
        if wsum <=0: break
        w=w/wsum
        g=np.zeros(n)
        for m,wt in zip(idx,w):
            lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
            if lo<=hi:
                g[lo:hi+1] += wt*2.0*best[m-hi:m-lo+1][::-1]
        g -= g.mean()
        gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
        if gmax < 1e-15:
            # random
            pass
        else:
            gnorm=g/gmax
            best_trial_f=best_f
            best_trial=None
            # step choices maybe dynamic
            for ss in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
                step=ss*step_scale
                a2=best*np.exp(-step*gnorm)
                s2=a2.sum()
                if s2<=1e-12: continue
                a2/=s2
                f2=_score_norm(a2); evals+=1
                if evals>=max_evals: break
                if f2 < best_trial_f - 1e-13:
                    best_trial_f=f2; best_trial=a2.copy()
            if best_trial is not None:
                best=best_trial; best_f=best_trial_f; fails=0; step_scale=min(4.0,step_scale*1.1)
                continue
        # random perturbation
        improved=False
        for scale in (0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
            a2=best*np.exp(scale*rng.normal(size=n))
            s2=a2.sum(); if s2<=... continue
            a2/=s2
            f2=_score_norm(a2); evals+=1
            if evals>=max_evals: break
            if f2 < best_f - 1e-12:
                best=a2.copy(); best_f=f2; fails=0; improved=True; break
        if not improved:
            fails +=1
            if fails > 30:
                # diversify current best: add random smooth noise
                noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,n))
                a2 = best*0.8 + 0.2*noise
                a2/=a2.sum(); f2=_score_norm(a2); evals+=1
                if f2 < best_f:
                    best=a2.copy(); best_f=f2; fails=0
                    step_scale=1.0
                else:
                    if fails > 200:
                        break
                    step_scale = rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)
    return best
```
We always use `best` for gradient and random, not current; greedy. If gradient fails, random around best. Might get stuck but okay.

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import time
import numpy as np
try:
    import scipy.optimize as sopt
except Exception:
    sopt = None

_PEN = 1e100

def _fft_len(need): ...

def _conv_full(a, threshold=256):
    n = a.size
    if n <= threshold:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
    need=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N<need: N <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:need]

def _score_norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim!=1 or a.size==0: return _PEN
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return _PEN
    b=_conv_full(a)
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0: return _PEN
    return 2.0*float(a.size)*m

def _normalize(a): ...

def _build_W(n,K): ...

def _de_obj_factory(n,K,holder): ...

def _run_de(obj,K,bounds, maxiter, popsize, seed): ...
```

    budget=max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    deadline=t0 + max(10.0, budget - 10.0)

    best_arr=np.array([1.0]); best_f=2.0
    exact_best_arr=np.array([1.0]); exact_best_score=float('inf')

```

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size ==0: return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0) # internal maybe normalize; clipping to 1000 no harm? If sum1 no. Use but be aware if a max>1000 and sum normalized impossible? We can skip clip before normalize for shape; but final must be ≤1000. Since scaling invariant, if shape has dynamic ratio causing max>1000 when sum maybe? We can scale. Let's not clip before normalize for internal; just finite nonneg. At final clip.

    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return
    a = a/s

def _scaled_versions(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=1e-12: return []
   arrs=[a/s]
   mx=a.max()
   if mx>0: 
      arrs.append(a/mx)
   for target in (10.0,100.0):
      if s>0: arrs.append(a*target/s)
   n=a.size
   arrs.append(a*np.sqrt(2.0*n)/s)
   # deduplicate by max
   out=[]
   for arr in arrs:
      arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
      if np.all(np.isfinite(arr)) and arr.sum()>=0.01:
          # if not close to existing based on max? just append
          out.append(arr)
   return out
```

```
class _SearchTimeout(Exception): pass

def _fft_len(need):
    N=1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    return N

def _conv_full(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    need = 2*n -1
    N=1
    while N < need: N <<=1
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:need]
    return out
```

`_score_norm` uses `np.max` on b. Could b contain tiny negative at all? no.

j = np.floor(t).astype(np.int64)
j = np.clip(j,0,K-1)
frac = t - j.astype(float)
# where j==K-1, frac might be tiny; set to0

W = np.zeros((n,K), dtype=np.float64)

np.add.at(W, (idx, j), 1.0 - frac)
j2 = np.minimum(j+1, K-1)

`_de_obj_factory`:
```
def _de_obj_factory(n,K,holder,v_holder,stop_time):
   W=_build_W(n,K)
   v_holder[0]=None
   need=2*n-1
   use_fft = n > 256
   N = _fft_len(need) if use_fft else 0
   def obj(v):
       if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
           raise _SearchTimeout()
       v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return _PEN
       a = W.dot(v)
       if a.size == 0: return _PEN
       np.maximum(a,0.0,out=a)
       s = float(a.sum())
       if s <= 1e-12: return _PEN
       a *= 1.0/s
       if use_fft:
          F = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
          b = np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:need]
       else:
          b = np.convolve(a,a)
       m = float(np.max(b))
       if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
          return _PEN
       f = 2.0 * float(n) * m
       if f < holder[0] - 1e-12:
          holder[0]=f
          holder[1]=a.copy()
          v_holder[0]=v.copy()
       return f
   return obj, W
```
Potential issue: `a *= 1.0/s` modifies a; if holder update copies after, good.

    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*K
    kwargs=...
    res=None
    try:
       try: res=sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, init='latinhypercube', **kwargs)
       except TypeError: res=sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, **kwargs)
    except _SearchTimeout:
       pass
    except Exception:
       pass
    return holder, v_holder, W, res

```python
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as sopt
except Exception:
    sopt = None

_PENALTY = 1e100
_fft_len_cache = {}


class _SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def _fft_len(need: int) -> int:
    L = _fft_len_cache.get(need)
    if L is None:
        L = 1
        while L < need:
            L <<= 1
        _fft_len_cache[need] = L
    return L


def _convolve_full(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Linear convolution a*a, using FFT for longer vectors."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 256:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = _fft_len(need)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:need]
    return out


def _score_normalized(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    Score 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) for a already non-negative with sum 1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return _PENALTY
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return _PENALTY
    b = _convolve_full(a)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return _PENALTY
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * m


def _normalize_nonnegative(a) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _build_interp_weights(n: int, K: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Dense weights W such that W @ v linearly interpolates coarse v onto n ordered points.
    """
    if K <= 1:
        return np.ones((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, float(K - 1), n)
    j = np.floor(t).astype(np.int64)
    j = np.clip(j, 0, K - 1)
    frac = t - j.astype(np.float64)
    frac = np.where(j == K - 1, 0.0, frac)

    W = np.zeros((n, K), dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n)
    W[idx, j] = 1.0 - frac
    nz = frac > 0.0
    if np.any(nz):
        j2 = np.minimum(j[nz] + 1, K - 1)
        W[idx[nz], j2] = frac[nz]
    return W


def _de_objective_factory(n, K, stop_time):
    holder_score = _PENALTY
    holder_a = None
    holder_v = None

    W = _build_interp_weights(n, K)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    use_fft = n > 256
    N = _fft_len(need) if use_fft else 1

    def objective(v):
        nonlocal holder_score, holder_a, holder_v
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
            raise _SearchTimeout()

        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            return _PENALTY

        a = W.dot(v)
        if a.size == 0:
            return _PENALTY

        np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return _PENALTY
        a /= s

        if use_fft:
            F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
            b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:need]
        else:
            b = np.convolve(a, a)

        m = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            return _PENALTY

        val = 2.0 * float(n) * m
        if val < holder_score - 1e-12:
            holder_score = val
            holder_a = a.copy()
            holder_v = v.copy()
        return val

    def getters():
        return holder_score, holder_a, holder_v, W

    return objective, getters


def _exact_score_list(lst) -> float:
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(lst)
    except Exception:
        return _PENALTY
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return _PENALTY
    return float(s)


def _scaled_versions(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return []
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return []

    outs = [a / s]

    mx = float(a.max())
    if mx > 0.0:
        outs.append(a / mx)

    for target in (10.0, 100.0):
        outs.append(a * (target / s))

    n = int(a.size)
    outs.append(a * (np.sqrt(2.0 * n) / s))

    valid = []
    for arr in outs:
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(arr)) and float(arr.sum()) >= 0.01:
            valid.append(arr)
    return valid


def _refine_unit(a0, stop_time, max_evals, seed):
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None or a.size < 4 or a.size > 10000:
        return np.asarray(a0).copy() if a0 is not None else np.ones(1)

    best = a.copy()
    best_f = _score_normalized(best)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n = int(best.size)
    step_scale = 1.0
    fails = 0
    evals = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time and evals < max_evals:
        b = _convolve_full(best)
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            break
        M = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
            break

        kact = min(8, int(b.size))
        if kact > 0:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -kact)[-kact:]
            w = b[idx].astype(np.float64)
            wsum = float(w.sum())
            if wsum > 1e-300:
                w /= wsum
                g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                for m, wt in zip(idx, w):
                    m = int(m)
                    lo = max(0, m - n + 1)
                    hi = min(n - 1, m)
                    if lo <= hi:
                        g[lo:hi + 1] += wt * 2.0 * best[m - hi:m - lo + 1][::-1]
                g -= float(g.mean())
                gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))

                if gmax > 1e-14:
                    gnorm = g / gmax
                    trial_best_f = best_f
                    trial_best_a = None

                    for ss in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
                        if evals >= max_evals or time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
                            break
                        step = ss * step_scale
                        a2 = best * np.exp(-step * gnorm)
                        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
                            continue
                        s2 = float(a2.sum())
                        if s2 <= 1e-12:
                            continue
                        a2 /= s2
                        f2 = _score_normalized(a2)
                        evals += 1
                        if np.isfinite(f2) and f2 < trial_best_f - 1e-13:
                            trial_best_f = f2
                            trial_best_a = a2.copy()

                    if trial_best_a is not None:
                        best = trial_best_a
                        best_f = trial_best_f
                        fails = 0
                        step_scale = min(4.0, step_scale * 1.15)
                        continue

        # Random multiplicative perturbation around current best.
        improved = False
        for scale in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40):
            if evals >= max_evals or time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
                break
            a2 = best * np.exp(scale * rng.normal(size=n))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
                continue
            s2 = float(a2.sum())
            if s2 <= 1e-12:
                continue
            a2 /= s2
            f2 = _score_normalized(a2)
            evals += 1
            if np.isfinite(f2) and f2 < best_f - 1e-12:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_f = f2
                fails = 0
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            fails += 1
            if fails == 60 or fails == 160:
                a2 = 0.70 * best + 0.30 * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, size=n))
                s2 = float(a2.sum())
                if s2 > 1e-12:
                    a2 /= s2
                    f2 = _score_normalized(a2)
                    evals += 1
                    if np.isfinite(f2) and f2 < best_f - 1e-12:
                        best = a2.copy()
                        best_f = f2
                        fails = 0
                        step_scale = 1.0
            if fails > 400:
                break
            step_scale = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 2.2))

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget <= 0:
        budget = 1300.0

    # Leave headroom for final exact validation and return.
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget - 5.0)
    de_stop = max(t0 + 1.0, deadline - 180.0)
    refine_stop = max(t0 + 1.0, deadline - 60.0)
    final_stop = deadline - 2.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Guaranteed valid fallback and internal best bookkeeping.
    best_arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    best_f = float(_score_normalized(best_arr))
    exact_best_arr = best_arr.copy()
    exact_best_score = _exact_score_list([1.0])

    stored = []

    def consider_array(a, force_exact=False):
        nonlocal best_arr, best_f, exact_best_arr, exact_best_score
        a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
        if a is None:
            return
        f = _score_normalized(a)
        if not np.isfinite(f):
            return

        if f < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_f = f
            best_arr = a.copy()

        n = int(a.size)
        if f < 1.80 or n <= 128 or (n >= 256 and f < 2.00) or len(stored) < 30:
            if len(stored) < 120:
                stored.append((f, a.copy(), n))
            elif f < (stored[-1][0] if stored else f) - 1e-3:
                stored[-1] = (f, a.copy(), n)

        # Exact-check small/forced candidates early; large candidates are checked at the end.
        if force_exact or n <= 512:
            for arr in _scaled_versions(a):
                sc = _exact_score_list([float(x) for x in arr.tolist()])
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_score:
                    exact_best_score = sc
                    exact_best_arr = arr.copy()

    # Use the supplied parent construction if available, without copying it literally.
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
        consider_array(parent, force_exact=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Cheap explicit windows.  These give non-uniform starts and protect against
    # a failing stochastic phase.
    if time.monotonic() < refine_stop - 30:
        for n in (64, 256, 1024, 4096):
            if time.monotonic() >= refine_stop - 30:
                break
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

            # Power windows.
            for p in (0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
                consider_array((1.0 - x) ** p)
                consider_array(x ** p)
                consider_array(1.0 - 0.5 * x ** p)

            # U-shaped and endpoint-emphasized windows.
            for c in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                consider_array(1.0 + c * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
                consider_array(1.0 + c * (x - 0.5) ** 3)

            # Smooth signature windows and antisymmetric signature windows.
            for e in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
                for f2 in (-0.4, 0.0, 0.4):
                    a = 1.0 + e * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + f2 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
                    consider_array(a)
                    a = 1.0 + e * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) + f2 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
                    consider_array(a)

            # Quadratic exponential windows.
            for b in (-3.0, -1.5, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.5, 3.0):
                for c in (-5.0, -2.0, 0.0, 2.0, 5.0):
                    a = np.exp(b * x + c * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
                    consider_array(a)

    # Coarse-parameter differential evolution, interpolated to the final resolution.
    if sopt is not None and time.monotonic() < de_stop:
        de_runs = [
            (10, 512, 1000, 8),
            (14, 1024, 1000, 8),
            (18, 2048, 1000, 8),
            (22, 4096, 1000, 6),
            (24, 6121, 1000, 6),
        ]

        run_seed = int(seed)
        for K, n, maxiter, popsize in de_runs:
            if time.monotonic() >= de_stop or n < K:
                continue

            try:
                obj, getters = _de_objective_factory(n, K, de_stop)
                bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] for _ in range(K)

                de_kwargs = dict(
                    maxiter=maxiter,
                    popsize=popsize,
                    mutation=(0.6, 1.2),
                    recombination=0.7,
                    seed=run_seed,
                    tol=1e-5,
                    polish=False,
                )
                try:
                    res = sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, init="latinhypercube", **de_kwargs)
                except TypeError:
                    res = sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, **de_kwargs)

                holder_score, holder_a, holder_v, W = getters()
                if holder_a is not None:
                    consider_array(holder_a)
                if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    consider_array(W.dot(res.x))
                if holder_v is not None:
                    for tn in (max(64, n // 2), min(8192, 2 * n)):
                        if tn != n and tn >= 2 * K:
                            W2 = _build_interp_weights(tn, K)
                            consider_array(W2.dot(holder_v))

            except _SearchTimeout:
                pass
            except Exception:
                pass

            run_seed += 101

        # If substantial time remains, repeat the most useful medium-size searches
        # with a different seed to sample a different part of the landscape.
        if time.monotonic() < de_stop - 60.0:
            de_runs2 = [
                (14, 1024, 1000, 8),
                (18, 2048, 1000, 8),
                (22, 4096, 1000, 6),
            ]
            for K, n, maxiter, popsize in de_runs2:
                if time.monotonic() >= de_stop:
                    break
                try:
                    obj, getters = _de_objective_factory(n, K, de_stop)
                    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] for _ in range(K)
                    de_kwargs = dict(
                        maxiter=maxiter,
                        popsize=popsize,
                        mutation=(0.7, 1.4),
                        recombination=0.75,
                        seed=run_seed + 917,
                        tol=1e-5,
                        polish=False,
                    )
                    try:
                        res = sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, init="sobol", **de_kwargs)
                    except TypeError:
                        try:
                            res = sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, init="latinhypercube", **de_kwargs)
                        except TypeError:
                            res = sopt.differential_evolution(obj, bounds, **de_kwargs)

                    holder_score, holder_a, holder_v, W = getters()
                    if holder_a is not None:
                        consider_array(holder_a)
                    if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                        consider_array(W.dot(res.x))
                except _SearchTimeout:
                    break
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Local refinement of the best reasonably-sized candidate.
    if time.monotonic() < refine_stop:
        large_candidates = [(f, a) for (f, a, n) in stored if 256 <= n <= 8192]
        if large_candidates:
            large_candidates.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
            refine_in = large_candidates[0][1]
        elif best_arr.size >= 256:
            refine_in = best_arr
        else:
            # If no useful candidate yet, use a mildly antisymmetric 1024-point seed.
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 1024)
            refine_in = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.3 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)

        n_ref = int(np.asarray(refine_in).size)
        if n_ref <= 512:
            eval_budget = 4000
        elif n_ref <= 1024:
            eval_budget = 2800
        elif n_ref <= 2048:
            eval_budget = 1700
        else:
            eval_budget = 900

        refined = _refine_unit(refine_in, refine_stop, eval_budget, seed=int(seed) + 1009)
        consider_array(refined, force_exact=True)

        # Also refine the current internal best if it is different and still timely.
        if (
            time.monotonic() < refine_stop - 10.0
            and best_arr.size != n_ref
            and 256 <= best_arr.size <= 8192
        ):
            n2 = int(best_arr.size)
            eb = 1500 if n2 <= 1024 else 800
            refined2 = _refine_unit(best_arr, refine_stop, eb, seed=int(seed) + 3001)
            consider_array(refined2, force_exact=True)

    # Final exact evaluation of top internal candidates and useful scalings.
    final_list = [1.0]
    final_score = _exact_score_list(final_list)

    candidate_arrays = []
    if best_arr is not None:
        candidate_arrays.append(best_arr)
    if exact_best_arr is not None:
        candidate_arrays.append(exact_best_arr)
    for _, a, _ in stored[:16]:
        candidate_arrays.append(a)

    # Try early-exit if deadline is very close.
    for a in candidate_arrays:
        if time.monotonic() >= final_stop - 5.0:
            break
        for arr in _scaled_versions(a):
            if time.monotonic() >= final_stop - 3.0:
                break
            sc = _exact_score_list([float(x) for x in np.asarray(arr).tolist()])
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = sc
                final_list = [float(x) for x in arr.tolist()]

    # If exact phase was cut short, still return the guaranteed fallback.
    if not final_list:
        final_list = [1.0]
    for x in final_list:
        if not np.isfinite(x):
            final_list = [1.0]
            break

    return final_list
```
